# Explicit console previews only. Creates one issue, never touches observation queues.
nfp_test_preview = {
 if = { limit = { is_player = yes }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = nfp_initialized } } nfp_initialize = yes }
  save_scope_as = nfp_reader
  save_scope_as = nfp_test_other
  random_country = { limit = { NOT = { this = scope:nfp_reader } } save_scope_as = nfp_test_other }
  random_scope_state = { save_scope_as = nfp_test_state }
  change_variable = { name = nfp_sequence add = 1 }

nfp_test_select = {
 set_variable = { name = nfp_test_number value = $number$ }
 if = { limit = { var:nfp_test_number = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp20_type value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp_section value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp_body value = flag:NFP20_DIVERGENCE }
 }
 else_if = { limit = { var:nfp_test_number = 2 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp20_type value = 2 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp_section value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp_body value = flag:NFP20_INFRA }
 }
 else_if = { limit = { var:nfp_test_number = 3 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp20_type value = 2 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp_section value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp_body value = flag:NFP20_ACCESS }
 }
 else_if = { limit = { var:nfp_test_number = 4 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp20_type value = 3 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp_section value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp_body value = flag:NFP46_SHORTAGE_BODY_1 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp20_problem value = 7 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp46_mode value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp50_supply value = 1 }
 }
 else_if = { limit = { var:nfp_test_number = 5 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp20_type value = 3 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp_section value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp_body value = flag:NFP46_SUPPLY_GAP_BODY_1 }

 }
 else_if = { limit = { var:nfp_test_number = 6 }

 }
 else_if = { limit = { var:nfp_test_number = 7 }

    set_variable = { name = nfp50_law_mode value = 0 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp50_law_gov_count value = 1 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp50_law_ig_count value = 2 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp50_law_gov_summary value = flag:NFP50_LAW_GOV_NEUTRAL }

nfp_test_election = {
 scope:nfp_reader = {
 ordered_active_party = { limit = { party_has_members = yes } order_by = momentum max = 6 check_range_bounds = no
  save_scope_as = nfp_test_party
  scope:nfp_new_article = {
   change_variable = { name = nfp43_rows add = 1 }
if = { limit = { var:nfp43_rows = 1 }    set_variable = { name = nfp43_party_1 value = scope:nfp_test_party }
    set_variable = { name = nfp43_now_1 value = 1.0 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp43_row_1 value = flag:NFP43_ELECTION_ROW_CURRENT_1 }
}
if = { limit = { var:nfp43_rows = 2 }    set_variable = { name = nfp43_party_2 value = scope:nfp_test_party }
    set_variable = { name = nfp43_now_2 value = 0.9 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp43_row_2 value = flag:NFP43_ELECTION_ROW_CURRENT_2 }
}
if = { limit = { var:nfp43_rows = 3 }    set_variable = { name = nfp43_party_3 value = scope:nfp_test_party }
    set_variable = { name = nfp43_now_3 value = 0.8 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp43_row_3 value = flag:NFP43_ELECTION_ROW_CURRENT_3 }
}
if = { limit = { var:nfp43_rows = 4 }    set_variable = { name = nfp43_party_4 value = scope:nfp_test_party }
    set_variable = { name = nfp43_now_4 value = 0.7 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp43_row_4 value = flag:NFP43_ELECTION_ROW_CURRENT_4 }
}
if = { limit = { var:nfp43_rows = 5 }    set_variable = { name = nfp43_party_5 value = scope:nfp_test_party }
    set_variable = { name = nfp43_now_5 value = 0.6 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp43_row_5 value = flag:NFP43_ELECTION_ROW_CURRENT_5 }
}
if = { limit = { var:nfp43_rows = 6 }    set_variable = { name = nfp43_party_6 value = scope:nfp_test_party }
    set_variable = { name = nfp43_now_6 value = 0.5 }
    set_variable = { name = nfp43_row_6 value = flag:NFP43_ELECTION_ROW_CURRENT_6 }
}
} } }
 if = { limit = { var:nfp43_rows = 0 }
set_variable = { name = nfp43_now_1 value = 1.0 }
set_variable = { name = nfp43_row_1 value = flag:NFPTEST_PARTY_1 }
set_variable = { name = nfp43_now_2 value = 0.9 }
